This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   This is an ML research experiment management session for the **distill_sparse_swiglu** project targeting EMNLP 2026 ARR (DDL: 2026-05-25). The Director's Round 18 Action Plan drives all work:
   
   - **Task D (HIGHEST PRIORITY)**: Per-layer KL training — 3 seeds (s42/s123/s456), 10000 steps each. s456 already complete. s42 and s123 restarted with intermediate checkpointing (--save_every 2000).
   - **Duplicate process cleanup**: Kill old training processes (no --save_every) competing with new ones on same GPUs.
   - **RevKL downstream eval verification**: Confirm all 3 seeds have complete 7-task metrics (they do).
   - **RevKL s42 BoolQ supplement**: Run BoolQ to align s42 with 7-task format (was missing BoolQ).
   - **Per-layer KL s456 PPL reproduction**: Verify PPL=9.0282 is stable/deterministic.
   - **Per-layer KL s456 BoolQ eval**: Align with RevKL 7-task format.
   - **Checkpoint mechanism verification**: Confirm --save_every 2000 produces checkpoint files at step 2000.
   - **Pre-authorized eval plan**: When per-layer KL training completes, immediately run PPL + 7-task downstream eval without waiting for Director.
   - **Eval environment preparation on westd-34887**: Ensure eval scripts, base model, and lm-eval harness are ready for s123 eval.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Per-layer KL training**: Each step randomly samples 1 layer, masks only that layer (others dense), forward entire model, compute KL(sparse_output, dense_output). Script: `train_perlayer_kl.py`.
   - **Coupling hierarchy**: KL (8.81) ≈ JSD (8.90) > Per-layer KL (9.03) > RevKL (9.26) >> TEAL (10.82) >> BCE (15.09) >> MSE (18.52)
   - **Registry state machine**: PREPARING → PENDING → QUEUED → ALLOCATED → RUNNING → ANALYZING → DONE. Cannot skip states.
   - **submit_training_job**: Uses tmux + cron monitoring. Commands MUST be wrapped in `bash -c "..."` for bash builtins. GPU numbering always starts from 0.
   - **AutoDL SCP limitation**: SCP between AutoDL instances is unreliable (hangs indefinitely). Eval should be scheduled on the server where checkpoints reside.
   - **Checkpoint sizes**: Full training state checkpoint (~455MB) includes optimizer, scheduler, lambdas, per-layer stats, RNG. Pure model weights ~151MB.
   - **Server architecture**: westd-34887 (port 34887) and westd-41718 (port 41718), both at connect.westd.seetacloud.com. Root account, conda at `/root/miniconda3/`.
   - **Worker API rate limit**: Workers cannot function until May 22, 6pm UTC. All operations done directly via submit_training_job/MCP tools.

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_14/projects/distill_sparse_swiglu/src/train_perlayer_kl.py`**
     - Core training script for per-layer KL experiment
     - Checkpoint save logic verified at line 274:
       ```python
       if args.save_every > 0 and (opt_step % args.save_every == 0 or opt_step == args.num_steps):
           ckpt_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, f"predictor_perlayer_kl_step_{opt_step}.pt")
           os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)
           torch.save({...})
       ```
     - `--save_every` argument at line 62: `p.add_argument("--save_every", type=int, default=2000, help="Save checkpoint every N steps (0=disabled)")`
     - `--resume` flag and resume logic included (from previous session modifications)
   
   - **`/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_14/projects/distill_sparse_swiglu/registry.yaml`** (~12500+ lines)
     - Key experiments tracked and updated throughout this session
     - Experiments modified: perlayer_kl_30_s42_v6 (→RUNNING), perlayer_kl_30_s123_v4 (→RUNNING), revkl_30_s456_downstream_v2 (→DONE), revkl_30_s42_boolq (→DONE), perlayer_kl_s456_ppl_repro (conclusion=success, metrics set), perlayer_kl_s456_boolq (conclusion=failed, SCP hung), perlayer_kl_s456_boolq_v2 (submitted)

   - **`/home/ubuntu/agent-ml-research/skills/global/resource-saturation.skill.md`**
     - Resource saturation self-check: GPU allocation rate, efficiency, worker pool requirements
     - Must include `[资源]` line in all Director reports

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **Registry state transition PREPARING → RUNNING invalid**:
     - Error: "Invalid transition: PREPARING → RUNNING. Allowed: ['PENDING', 'QUEUED']"
     - Fix: Step through full state machine PREPARING → PENDING → QUEUED → ALLOCATED → RUNNING
   
   - **Duplicate training processes on both servers**:
     - Two python processes sharing each GPU (old without --save_every + new with --save_every), severely slowing training
     - Fix: Killed old processes (PID 326200 on 41718, PID 282634 on 34887), verified with nvidia-smi
     - Director feedback: "这是第三次重复进程了。以后启动训练前必须先 `pgrep -af 'train.*seed'` 检查。"
   
   - **AutoDL SCP between instances hung**:
     - SCP from westd-41718 to westd-34887 hung for 16+ minutes with no progress
     - Fix: Killed the stuck job, resubmitted eval on westd-41718 where checkpoint is local
     - Lesson: "AutoDL 实例间 SCP 不可靠，以后 eval 安排在 checkpoint 所在服务器"
   
   - **read_registry filter-by-exp_id returning empty** (from previous session):
     - Workaround: Used grep on registry.yaml directly or used limit parameter without exp_id filter

5. Problem Solving:
   - **Duplicate processes**: Diagnosed via `ps aux | grep train_perlayer`, confirmed both processes using ~35GB VRAM each on same GPU. Old processes at ~1000 steps (59min with contention), well below 3000 threshold → killed old, kept new.
   - **s42 v5 mystery**: Director asked about perlayer_kl_30_s42_v5.log showing step 9700/10000 on westd-34887. Investigation confirmed: log is historical (no matching PID in ps output), process already dead.
   - **Checkpoint verification**: Both servers confirmed step_2000 checkpoints (455,937,147 bytes each) after training passed step 2000. Code logic verified correct.
   - **PPL reproducibility**: s456 PPL repro returned exactly 9.0282, confirming deterministic eval.
   - **BoolQ results collected**: RevKL s42 BoolQ = 65.29%, Per-layer KL s456 BoolQ = 51.25% (Director noted this as "important finding").
